Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the banach space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 308 and the convergence rate is O(297/n^1).

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by banach generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 308, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 297. QED.

Theorem 211.5 (Spectral Property)
